**[7320.00s] English:** He murders the president of the United States, and then, less than 48 hours later, that lone gunman is himself murdered by another lone gunman.

**[7346.48s] English:** This bizarre chain of killings was all entirely natural.  
**Translation:** 

**[7350.72s] English:** So, less than a year after the JFK assassination, the Johnson White House released something called the Warren Commission Report.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: assassination: 刺杀

**[7357.42s] English:** And the report concluded that while their motives remained unclear, both Lee Oswald and Jack Ruby had acted alone.  
**Translation:** 

**[7363.82s] English:** No one helped them. There was no conspiracy of any kind.  
**Translation:** 

**[7367.08s] English:** Case closed. Time to move on.  
**Translation:** 

**[7369.40s] English:** And many Americans did move on.  
**Translation:** 

**[7371.48s] English:** At the time, they had no idea how shoddy and corrupt the Warren Commission was.  
**Translation:** 

**[7375.44s] English:** It would be nearly 50 years before the CIA admitted under duress that, in fact,  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: duress: 逼迫; shoddy: 低质

**[7380.10s] English:** it had withheld information from investigators about its relationship with Lee Harvey Oswald.  
**Translation:** 

**[7386.32s] English:** But even then, at the time, before that was known, the government's explanation didn't seem entirely plausible.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: investigators: 调查人员; plausible: 合乎情理; withheld: 隐瞒

**[7392.00s] English:** And some people started asking obvious questions about it.  
**Translation:** 

**[7395.46s] English:** It was at that point, as Americans started to doubt the official story, that the term conspiracy theory entered our lexicon.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: lexicon: 词汇库

**[7402.18s] English:** As Professor Lance de Haven Smith points out in his book on the subject,  
**Translation:** 

**[7406.26s] English:** the term conspiracy theory did not exist as a phrase in everyday American conversation.  
**Translation:** 

**[7410.28s] English:** The term conspiracy theory was established in 1964.  
**Translation:** 

**[7412.36s] English:** In 1964, the year the Warren Commission issued its report,  
**Translation:** 

**[7416.64s] English:** the New York Times published five stories in which conspiracy theory appeared.  
**Translation:** 

**[7421.62s] English:** Now today, of course, the term conspiracy theory appears in pretty much every New York Times story about American politics.  
**Translation:** 

**[7428.06s] English:** It's wielded, now as then, as a weapon against anyone who asks questions the government doesn't feel like answering.  
**Translation:** 

**[7434.82s] English:** But despite 60 years of name-calling, those questions have not disappeared.

**[7459.52s] English:** Those are conclusions that, puzzlingly, no one who had spoken to Jack Ruby previously had reached.
